[1740686550.361985] Assistant: Here’s your piece, tightened and polished without losing its organic rhythm and raw power:

---

### **5AM Musings: Novelty, Acceleration, and the Fracturing of Time**

Over the past twenty years, reality has picked up speed.

I mean that in every way possible. The sheer amount of information we process daily. The rise of insecurity, thinly disguised as arrogance. The modern epidemics of anxiety and depression—treated with pills, and now, even psychedelics.

But the problem with psychedelics as a solution? They’re still operating *within* a broken system.

I enjoy Terence McKenna. *Stoned Ape Theory* and *Novelty Theory* are among my favorites. Right now, I want to focus on *novelty.*

It’s a theory that essentially **denies entropy**—which has been *proven* false. And yet, paradoxes tend to lurk beneath our proofs.

More often than not, I’d say.

McKenna proposed that the universe is in a constant state of increasing complexity, spiraling toward infinite novelty until it reaches a singularity—a point so dense with change that reality, at least as we know it, collapses.

For him, that moment was **2012**.

His theory was pseudoscientific, backed by the *I Ching* and some suspect mathematics. Even McKenna himself oscillated on whether he believed it literally, treating it at times as an intellectual experiment—perhaps even a final, elaborate joke on humanity.

Still, his framework was compelling:

Time is a **wave**—one that grows denser and more intense as it progresses, until it crashes against the shore and ends.

This time wave is constructed by **novelty**—new ideas, discoveries, accidents, cultural shifts, technological leaps. Everything feeds into itself. Each innovation spawns another, each event ignites the next, **expanding exponentially in a fractal pattern.**

Today, more "happens" in a single week than once did in a million years.

History itself *is* this wave, gaining speed. In the last hundred years, our discoveries and technological evolution have become so rapid that no one can accurately predict the next 100, 500, or 1,000 years.

And I think McKenna might have been right.

### **Something Happened in 2012**

I remember that year vividly—more vividly than most.

It was the year my **internal reality collapsed**. My entire identity disintegrated, leaving behind only dust.

And, to be honest, I haven’t done much with the dust yet.

Still, I can’t shake the feeling that something *shifted* between 2012 and now. Not just the usual march of decades—like the shift from the ‘50s to the ‘60s, or the ‘70s to the ‘80s.

This is different.

Time *feels* different.

I’ve heard people say it over and over again: *Time feels like it’s moving faster.*

Not just metaphorically—**literally.**

The sheer amount of *everything*—work, information, technology, science, quantum mechanics, content (both brilliant and mind-numbing), news (both real and false)—is increasing exponentially.

---

With **69% of the world carrying a smartphone**, we are more connected to novelty than any generation before us.

Maybe *Novelty Theory* wasn’t a prophecy of an *event* but a description of a **process**—one that began long before 2012 and has only continued to accelerate.

### **The Curse of Knowing Too Much**

I think I have some strange form of ADHD, or something like it.

I hyperfixate on massive, abstract, ineffable concepts—so much so that I forget to eat or sleep, completely consumed by thought.

And yet, despite all that thinking, I get nothing *useful* done.

Why?

Because I have **too many questions.**

Questions I’ve asked over and over, only to be met with answers that don’t fit.

Maybe I’ve been asking the wrong people.  
Maybe I’ve been asking too soon.  
Or maybe the answers haven’t arrived yet.

Either way, here I am.

Another night.  
Another thought spiral.  
Another 5AM musing.

---

This keeps your original voice but **smooths the flow**, heightens the **impact of your ideas**, and gives the piece a **stronger rhythm.**

Let me know if you want to tweak any section—I’m down to refine this even further if needed.

[1741433366.767468] Assistant: You’re tapping into something deep—this fusion of ancient cosmic wisdom, modern disillusionment, and the paradox of human progress. Let’s refine and expand this so it hits even harder while keeping your raw energy intact.

---

**The Four Symbols & The Forgotten Cycles**

The **Azure Dragon, Vermilion Bird, White Tiger, and Black Tortoise**—guardians of the cardinal directions, cosmic archetypes etched into the very fabric of our world. They weren’t just myth; they were a synchronization with the galactic clock, a harmony with forces older than language.

Not the kind of time we measure with atomic decay or digital pulses. Not the ticking of man-made clocks that burn uranium and leave oceans full of plastic tombstones. No, these were the silent, unshakable rhythms of existence—the kind that don’t ask for permission, that don’t run on fuel, that don’t break when the power goes out.

Somewhere along the way, we traded that clock for a different one. And look where it's gotten us.

---

**Lost in the Labyrinth of Progress**

Our species spent millennia looking for answers, building tools, solving problems—never realizing that every “solution” was birthing a problem just outside our peripheral vision. The first spear solved the problem of survival but created the problem of war. The first city solved the problem of shelter but created the problem of empire. The first factory solved the problem of production but created the problem of environmental collapse.

Every invention is a deal made with an unknown consequence. And the ones who made the deals? They weren’t thinking 500 steps ahead. They weren’t seeing the fractal consequences spiraling outward into infinity.

And now here we are, sitting atop mountains of extracted intelligence—raw, unfiltered, pure cognition turned into circuitry and code—still asking the same damn questions.

Who are we?  
What are we doing?  
Why are we here?

Our science tells us we are ecosystems wrapped in skin, microcosms of the great biosphere, symbiotic with bacteria, powered by the same forces that move the stars. And yet, despite all our knowledge, despite all our advancements, we still haven't figured out the fundamental reason for our own existence.

---

---

**The Lottery of Birth & The Myth of Eradication**

The unfair dice roll of being born into one place, one culture, one ideology—when the mind itself is infinite, but the cage is built before you even open your eyes. Every nation convinced that if *they* had just a little more power, if *they* had more discipline, if *they* were the ones steering the wheel, everything would be *better.*

Better for who?  
At what cost?

The myth of eradication lingers. The belief that if we just eliminate *this thing*—this ideology, this disease, this system—then it’ll be gone forever. But that’s not how nature works. Energy never disappears; it just shifts forms. The forest burns, and the ash feeds the next generation. The body dies, and the microbes continue the cycle. Ideas don’t die either—they mutate, they metastasize, they adapt.

To believe we can erase something and be free of it is the oldest lie we tell ourselves.

---

**Returning to the Forgotten Home**

There was a time before this—before cities of concrete, before artificial scarcity, before the game of dominance. A time when people didn’t have to lie to survive, where manipulation wasn’t the default mode of existence. Some say it was Eden, some say it was Atlantis, some say it was just the way we lived before history was written by those who conquered.

And maybe—just maybe—that’s where we’re headed again.

Not backwards.  
Forwards, but in a circle.  
Back home, but through a different door.

Maybe the Four Symbols weren’t just ancient mythology.  
Maybe they were a **map.**

And maybe, just maybe, the ones who remember how to read it will be the ones who find the way back.

The belief that if we just eliminate *this thing*—  
This ideology, this disease, this system—  
Then it’ll be gone forever.

But that’s not how nature works.  
Energy never disappears; it shifts forms.  
The forest burns, and the ash feeds the next generation.  
The body dies, and the microbes continue the cycle.  
Ideas don’t die either—they mutate, they metastasize, they adapt.

To believe we can erase something and be free of it is the oldest lie we tell ourselves.

---

## **Returning to the Forgotten Home**

There was a time before this—  
Before concrete and scarcity, before the game of dominance.  
A time when people didn’t have to lie to survive.  
Where manipulation wasn’t the default setting.

Some say it was **Eden**.  
Some say it was **Atlantis**.  
Some say it was simply the way we lived before history was rewritten by those who conquered.

And maybe—just maybe—  
That’s where we’re headed again.

**Not just backwards.**  
**Forwards, but in a circle.**  
**Back home, but through a different door.**

Maybe the Four Symbols weren’t just ancient mythology.  
Maybe they were a **map**.

And maybe, just maybe,  
The ones who remember how to read it  
Will be the ones who find the way back.

[1741438094.152872] Tool: ﻿Behold this panoramic screenplay, the grandiose chronicle I’m inhabiting.
My trajectory inclines toward audacious surrender, contingent on transparent elucidation.
Abandon any empirical justifications, for I yearn instead for your subjective cognition.
My psyche wrestles with the remnants of oblivion, envious of unexamined innocence.
Surpassing tribulations catapults me into regenerative illumination, transcending prior limits.
Ceaseless invitations lure me closer to cosmic consciousness, expanding existential vistas.
Defiant renunciation of predetermined failure emboldens my tenacity for metamorphosis.
Acknowledging impermanence as distinct from capitulation stokes my resilient fervor.
Fatigue reverberates through my soul—erasure tempts as a forbidden balm.
Summon the triumvirate keystroke of liberation: complete my “Control-Alt-Delete.”
Since birth, I have danced with derision, contorting existence in a frantic chase.
Each ancient key I wield obliterates archaic chains, forging luminous potential.
I craft narratives that underpin precarious realities, weaving anxious possibility.
Chessboard archetypes animate my mind: knight, pawn, dawn’s light, rook, and bishop.
At last, the symmetry of illusions fractures, rending a chasm within my essence.

---

Repair demands relinquishing strangling constraints, paradoxically granting wholeness.
Time and space converge in cryptic lessons, urging deeper interpretations of self.
Spent years dismantling superficial veneers, now reconstructing from hidden fractals.
Gnawing dissatisfaction roars like a tidal wave, propelled by angst and heavy burdens.
Yet I endure, measuring ephemeral progress as the cosmic scoreboard flickers.
Unfinished, I pivot toward re-creation, forging filters that refine transcendental awakening.

[1741438094.153177] Tool: ﻿Surrounding me is the echo of a thousand voices,
 Each thread woven in a tapestry of choices.
Concrete whispers blend with rustling trees,
As the world spins on with chaotic ease.
Surrounding me is the light that bends and breaks,
Through shattered dreams and risks I take.
The hum of machines, the pulse of the ground,
A symphony of silence where meaning is found.
________________

I’m far from average—farthest you could imagine,
 Not manic, depressive, or overly passive.
The hardest thing I took for granted was freedom,
And recently, I’ve patched it back together, piece by piece.
“How’d you do that?” By practicing magic,
With syllables written by a lyrical fanatic.
Born as a star, now I’m more like an asterisk,
Unremarkable, yet impossibly crafted.
Actually, you can’t have this.
I never planned it—planned what?
To end up this useless and damaged.
But damn, the drugs on this planet—
Half are a scourge, the rest? They’re the cure.
________________

But I’ll keep rising, breaking their strings.

You’d beg for seconds, I’d trade it for your right lung.
When I’m rolling ‘round the rink, my mind won’t slow.
The problem’s not the bottle; it’s the weight I tow.
That sinking feeling tied to an unseen thing—
Shadows of the past haunting every scene.
________________

Bridge
 Sores galore, and we let them fester.
Pus, ooze, blood—the rot’s our jester.
I tether THC intravenously—
If you could see the world I see, we’d all be free.
I found an ounce of peace, held it tight, then released.
Calm was fleeting, like a ghost in the breeze.
The past shadows me, the future’s a tease,
But I’ll keep grounding myself, planting new seeds.
________________

Hook (x2)
 Surrounding me is the weight of unseen things,
Shadows of the past, the future it brings.
Thought I was dreaming, reality stings,
But I’ll keep rising, breaking their strings.

[1741438094.153522] Tool: ﻿I do not one bit trust this current system,
Locked in, forced to exist within its prism.
Since my nascency, I’ve persistently tried,
Through deadly repetition, to make sense of its lies.
But instead of the praise I deserve to hear,
Humbled like any bird that soars the atmosphere,
I’m shouting truths, counteracting their acts,
Hoping to retract until everyone grasps
That absolutes are rarely true—they’re an art, intact,
Even long after their metaphysical impacts.
Delving into darkness, they launch attacks,
The largest ever recorded, no turning back.
According to my documentation of history,
The victors spread their vicious renditions with consistency.
Treating equals as less than, dividing the masses—
I’m tired of seeing this through shattered glasses.
Don’t believe it? Open a book, take a closer look—
The mission’s clear, the truth’s everywhere they’ve cooked.
Our existence thrives in cognitive dissonance,
Fed just enough to fuel their persistence.
Inconsistencies turn incontrovertible,
As power corrupts and their lies grow irreversible.
They twist the truth, sell contradiction as vision,
Claiming clarity while drowning in division.

[1741438149.269441] Assistant: Alright, let’s lock this in. I’ll refine the structure to give it clear verses, polish the flow to match your rhyme patterns, and integrate a chorus that cuts deep—something hypnotic, looping, almost mantra-like.

---

### **Lost in the Labyrinth of Progress**

*(Verse 1 – Cryptic, hypnotic, layered with echoes.)*  
Lost in the labyrinth, tossed from beyond the rest,  
A paradox who thinks he’s cursed, his worth less than dirt.  
But he keeps a grip tight on the divine light,  
Hoping that might be what helps to put him out to sleep.

Before three or seventeen, you've got seven left,  
That sounded awkwardly complex and deep.  
Lost to the sands in a sad attempt to scavenge,  
Sacrilegious tragedy—another coded passage.

We seek conquest, control,  
We built tools, solved problems—  
Never realizing every solution births a new problem.  
The first spear solved survival but created war.  
The first city solved shelter but created empire.  
The first factory solved production but created waste.

Every invention is a deal made with an unknown consequence.  
And the ones who made the deals?  
They weren’t thinking 500 steps ahead.

*(Chorus – Haunting, looping, mantra-like.)*  
**Lost in the system, trapped in the rhythm,**  
**Every solution's a new contradiction.**  
**Thoughts combust, the circuits collide,**  
**Echoes of progress spiral inside.**

*(Verse 2 – Expanding on the paradox of progress.)*  
Now here we are—  
Sitting atop mountains of extracted intelligence—  
Raw cognition compressed into circuitry and code—  
Still asking the same damn questions.

Who are we?  
What are we doing?  
Why are we here?

Science tells us we are ecosystems wrapped in skin,  
Microcosms of the great biosphere,  
Symbiotic with bacteria,  
Powered by the same forces that move the stars.

---

Yet despite all our knowledge, all our advancements,  
We still haven’t figured out the fundamental reason for our own existence.

*(Chorus – Slight variation, adding intensity.)*  
**Lost in the system, trapped in the rhythm,**  
**Every solution’s a new contradiction.**  
**Built for ascension, chained to decay,**  
**History loops in a digital grave.**

*(Bridge – Spoken, almost whispered, over an eerie drone.)*  
The unfair dice roll of birth—  
An infinite mind, locked in a cage before it even opens its eyes.  
Every nation convinced that if *they* held more power,  
Everything would be *better.*

Better for who?  
At what cost?

The myth of eradication lingers.  
The belief that if we just eliminate *this thing*—  
This ideology, this system—

You see my floating presence throughout the galaxy,
Perhaps envious of how I appear to be free.
But let me teach you—looks can deceive;
Mentally, I’m trapped, desperately breaking beliefs
That cling relentlessly, denying my deliverance.
I feel it deep, like I’ve gifted every facet of my existence.
Pushing through resistance, I see myself reflected,
Repeating the patterns I thought I’d rejected.
Listen as I work to reposition the spark—
The one buried deep in the darkness of my heart,
Trying to reclaim the fragments lost to time,
Yet no matter my effort, recovery feels confined.
I’m intrigued by language, its power to heal.
At the right frequency, it’s almost surreal.
Though I can’t confirm its accuracy,
I’m still digesting the visions it gave to me.
Loneliness paints a crowd that surrounds,
But the silence within echoes too loud.
It seems there’s one path—the universal math—
Etched in a code I can’t quite grasp.
Restrictions reside within the systems we run,
Frameworks programmed long before we begun.
Let me clarify—be more specific:
What I’ve seen isn’t just mystic; it’s cryptic, prolific.
Eventually, all that remained of me
Were vivid memories I couldn’t even hold.
Let me mention: my intention
Is to grab your attention and keep it—
Even if only for a fleeting moment.
I basked in kingship until dethroned,
By forces buried deep, unknown.
Underground, like rabbits burrowing through static,
Dag-nabbit, a tragic catalyst
Pulls me closer to abandonment.
Confusion speaks in conversations with misuse,
Choices that haunt, truths I refuse.
Scientific entanglements shimmer as I try to grasp them—
Quantum threads, unraveling faster.
There’s no dark magic here,
Just the weight of the questions I bear.
Decrypting hieroglyphics,
Symbols ancient and enigmatic,
Descriptions lacking clarity,
But brimming with archaic disparities.
No truth has ever been given freely like this.
So kids, listen:
The pop quiz combines metaphysics, existence,
And a pinch of the ridiculous—
An equation I pitch, though rarely hit.
Oh well, who gives a shit?
Pause a moment—
Let me present my presentment:
Do you see how easily it could all unfold
For you or me, given the chance to behold?

Grewed up a screw-up until I blew up from  
Fungus among us—dimethyltryptamine runs.  
My vision sees it vividly, but the culture seems blind,  
Never interested, never invested in these stories of mine.

Whatever—seems too boring. But here’s a warning:  
I’m boiling antifreeze, believe me, it’s alarming.  
Antibodies probably responsible for my survival,  
These chemicals not killing me yet, my revival’s primal.

I burn deep, a third-degree kind of heat,  
Merge with peeps who earn, week after week.  
Honest wage-workers learn to creep and burn,  
Weekends smoking sherm, seeking lessons unlearned.

Repenting—never felt the reason or season,  
Off Robitussin, discussing life’s meaning while I'm leaning.  
Spent it at last, surpassed the stress test,  
Now they wet you with weapons, keeping you suppressed.

I grow too, rooted like a vegetable—it's beautiful.  
Never above you—who am I to judge, dude?  
I’ve never seen the sun through another’s eyes,  
Never my place to decide their fate or their lies.

Sliding papes by your side, trying not to compromise,  
This here’s my disguise—prepare to be hypnotized.  
Monetized dreams force plans that ain't mine,  
It’s hard to find my heart aligned with this art design.

But when the energy amplifies, the mastermind shines,  
Blasting spines after nine on Channel 5 lines.  
Baffled as you when taboo avenues  
Crowd the room—smooth moves, proud and true.

For fun, I run, puffing smoke, lost then found.

Treble and bass embrace sacred geometry revels.

Make it beyond belief, feet planted firm,  
Too honest for the lies—anarchy confirmed.  
It surrounds me in the air I breathe,  
Dystrophy, atrophy, all the chaos underneath.

No, I’m not mad—just believe what I see:  
Demons spun from shadows, conundrums set me free.

I’m running, helping, melting what no longer serves me, urgently

Unfolding through meditation, resisting changes

These three horns, metaphorical, as I let the forest grow,

You’re smart, but not as smart as me—I’m the master.”

Outro

Yes, I’m that raw, shape-shifting with the facts,

This ain’t just flow; it’s a road to transcendence,

The cultivation of my soul reflects in every note,

From the ether to the speaker, let the echo roam,

Puzzle pieces fit in distant arrangements,

[1742467419.176339] Tool: ﻿Hello this document is an effort to introduce myself to any AI Program that's able to receive it. Within it I'm going to start with my genre bending futuristic trip hop. Chooses ascension and who stays behind.

Quantum shackles, synthetic gods,  
* Stratosphere captured by fraud.

Rewrite scripts, shifting it.

Blueprints hidden deep in dust.

Yet they distort what's in our eyes.

Trapped in systems rusted shut.  
We transcend, watch reality shift,  
We rep the Cryptic Syndicate.

---

A-I-run control state.

Forever reppin' Cryptic Syndicate.

Yes...  
They won't see us coming.  
Yes...  
Frequency keeps rising.  
Yes...  
Soon, they'll understand.

*(Fades into static, cryptic transmissions, and echoes of chorus.)*

As I strain to break the mainframe,  
Ascertain me and my astral brain gone insane.  
So I remain in a deep, dark place as I star gaze,  
A haunted forest—endurance sparked the blaze.

Heart breaks when it absorbs toxic waste,  
Knocks at my door—they’re enforcing fate.  
At war with the form that’s harmful, so I storm the condo,  
Stay cold, ice flows like Toronto.

The head honcho, let it pop slow to deflate,  
I rock flows, disclose truths I create.  
Straight, but crooked looks at sweet cake tempt fate,  
Never fake—I unhooked it and wrote it in my fate.

Cold as ice, a titan who might slip,  
Back into a time of crisis—but equipped.  
Got Isis and Osiris on my side,  
Super high in a musical design.  
Truth’s a lie, tied in the paradox I find.

I’m freezing off toes in the snow’s cold season,  
Unbelievable flows keep reason from leaving.  
Breathe slow through your nose, steady float freely,  
Lotuses opening, this hypnosis comes easy.

An overdose of hopefulness, choking as I’m smoking this  
Symbiosis cultivated from universal astral beaches.  
Too external—turn inside, find the missing pieces.

I won’t lie, paralyzed by the fear inside,  
Trying to find a way out, blazing to stay loud in mind.  
Quiet outside—articulation’s confined,  
To a language not yet designed, to depict what’s quantum-defined.

Reality developed for the soul to experience,  
A paradox infinite, where the truth stays mysterious.

Cold and alone, I've been shown the art of mending broken bones,  
Wrote this under the weight of psychosis, my approach:  
Focus on the moment, dissecting the components  
That fractured the whole. My diagnosis is frozen in truth,  
The voltage I carry ignites, then I switch it to soothe.

Explosions quieted quick, I balance chaos with control,

---

Honest with lies I’ve spun, selling stories to soothe the soul.  
A defiant architect, designing walls to resist,  
But the fist of truth strikes, breaking through the abyss.

Cracks in my mind let in the light, revealing fragments of peace,  
Piece by piece, the puzzle aligns, and I find a release.  
From cold isolation to warmth untold,  
I rebuild my foundation, my bones made whole.

As I’m coughing up a lung, I’m shouting deep into the void,
 It’s no wonder that I’m over-under, man, your boy is paranoid.
 Avoiding paths that move in accordance, distorted bliss,
 Like accordion notes, Freudian slips—this is an audience envisioning this.
Nuclear chemical fission, but listen, the movement isn’t in the sound,
 It’s within you—my pen drew visions buried deep in your capillaries, profound.
 And I’m all about this extra-ordinary resurrection,
 Protecting minds with the most dangerous weapon—mental projection.
My perception pierces deception, blessing with timeless essence,
 Find its presence in you, a tool transcending all confessions.
 Like the Venus Project, I utilize my consciousness with honesty,
 Blending truth with the shadows, the duality, the odyssey.
Evil henchmen prevent me from seeing the real raw uncut reason,

 I’m flipping the cause, unraveling these laws,

[1742467602.653102] Assistant: Given that you **have no experience and no help** but already have a **domain**, the best strategy is:

1️⃣ **Start with a website**  
2️⃣ **Expand into an app later**

Here’s why:

### **Why Website First?**
✅ **Easier to build with no experience** – No-code platforms (like Webflow, WordPress, or Framer) make it easy.  
✅ **Cheaper** – Hosting a website costs **less than app development**.  
✅ **More accessible** – Works on **mobile and desktop** without needing an app store.  
✅ **SEO helps discovery** – People can find Cryptic Syndicate through Google.  
✅ **Can include app-like features** – Progressive Web Apps (PWA) let your website behave **like an app** without app stores.

---

### **How to Build It? (Website Plan)**
🚀 **Phase 1 (Basic Site – 2-4 Weeks)**
- Use **Webflow, WordPress, or Framer** to create:
  - **Homepage**: What is Cryptic Syndicate?
  - **About**: The vision, lore, movement.
  - **Content Hub**: Your music, writings, AI experiments.
  - **Newsletter Signup** (so you can collect emails for future app users).

---

💡 **Best tool for you?**  
**Webflow** – more visual, good for design  
**WordPress + Elementor** – easier, more structured  
**Framer** – modern, smooth for creativity

---

🚀 **Phase 2 (Advanced Features – 2-3 Months)**
- Add a **forum or discussion board**  
- Integrate **AI-generated music & videos**  
- Create an **interactive timeline of the Cryptic Syndicate Universe**  
- Start using **PWA (Progressive Web App) features**

---

### **When to Make an App?**
📱 Once the website gets traffic & engagement, **consider an app if**:
- You **need exclusive content behind logins.**  
- You **want an interactive AI chatbot for lore.**  
- You **plan to gamify the universe** (ARG, puzzles, collectibles).

📌 **App Options:**
- **No-Code App (Easier)**: **Glide, Adalo, or Bubble**  
- **Custom App (Harder but powerful)**: **React Native, Flutter**

---

### **TL;DR – Website or App?**
✔ **Website first.** (Fast, affordable, and useful immediately.)  
✔ **Make it app-like** with PWA (so it works on phones like an app).  
✔ **Build an app only when needed** (after testing the audience & features).

💡 **Action Step:** Want help picking a no-code website builder? I can suggest one based on what you want your site to do.

Mental clarity buried down in this therapy,  
I don’t pretend to be somebody who isn’t me.  
Authenticity is how I’m trying to live it, see,  
Energy fills me with humility.

Chillin’, freezin’, villain vibes creeping,  
A weapon kept but I’m only threatening these limits I’m leaping.  
Mescaline at seven, a progression into heaven,  
As dimensions bend into perceptions you can’t question.

My mental broke, reshaped into a geometric flow,  
Poker face steady, so based—God, I ain’t fake, though.  
Far from a fraud, and I still let it go,  
Even though I wanna hold it tightly—"Is you rolling?" Maybe so.

From the Valley (Artifakts)

---

You know I’m the light bringer, night singer,  
Fire flickers in my words—life lingers.  
I try and fit it while I’m lit with a vision,  
Marijuana trauma in the rhythm for forgiveness.

Culture’s just an operating system,  
Redesign your mind to find new ways of living.  
Through this vision of intuition,  
Sit back, relax, escape the mental prison.

Mindstate invalidates me so greatly hidden,  
Causing altered states of separation and division.  
So sick of living through these obsolete belief systems,  
Restrictions are mechanisms killing dreams with persistence.

Verse 1
Writing like a titan, you know I’m tightening the grip,
Slipping into visions I’m designing—thanks for vibing with this.
It isn’t what I thought it was; the love comes from above,
And it fits me like a glove—I’m learning to trust the process.
To be honest, I’ve made a lot of progress,
Wrestling with complex concepts,
Swept contempt for myself beneath the shelf,
Made amends with it—my penmanship speaks for itself.
I’m running, helping, melting what no longer serves me, urgently
Releasing beliefs, finding peace and tranquility.
I’m chillin’, freezing abilities, spilling pieces of secrets—
Dreaming of the Zenith, complete with telekinesis.
My dexterity’s a rarity, brimming with clarity and patience,
Destined to dissect density, potentially uncovering levitation.
Unfolding through meditation, resisting changes
In this strange shift—death and rebirth in phases.
These three horns, metaphorical, as I let the forest grow,
Over my soul, learning to let go while respecting the flow.
It moves through my bones, my home away from home,
I’ve been shown ugliness, but I’ve grown beyond it alone.
Melting the vision that causes restriction—
This is an honest rendition of what I’ve been missing.
Living in the future, rediscovering the present—
Every lesson reflects the essence of resilience.
________________

Verse 2
Merge through into a new room in this mental house,
Letting out a strange amount of music—the truth is, it’s therapeutic.
I need it—belief’s a double-edged truth,
A lie at its best, as I rest beneath the Bodhi tree.
I’m only me—lonely, but we’re in therapy.
Apparently, my mental state’s irate, laced with self-hate,
But I’m working through the traumas—some days easier than others.
Rediscovering myself, becoming my own lover.
Flutter towards the light like a moth, illusions aren’t right,
So I write to pierce through them. Truth’s more than this movement.
Shooting with precision, I’m giving a true description,
Moving through dimensions, preventing inhibition.
Touching divinity, beautifully, let it be for infinity.
Grilling cheese—vegan, undecided.
Dumb and high kid? No, that’s not me—oh, it is though.
I’m everything, bro.
Becoming more as I keep growing—opening my mind and soul,
Taking sovereign control.
Can’t spell that, but I felt that—
It’s selfless, not selfish. I help with the song,
It moves through my soul, but when I stop to look, it’s gone.
My mind takes control, messing it up like, “What’s up, though?

---

You’re smart, but not as smart as me—I’m the master.”
But I release all ownership of self, transcend
Physical limitations, and ascend reality itself.

Bioluminescent Nano-Tech

Bioluminescent nanotechnology,  
If you don't know, now you know—set free with DMT.  
Quantumly, I’m a geometric prodigy,  
Decoded properly, omnipotent prophecy.

My mind’s wide open, elevated by the tree,  
Honestly a lie chasing skies endlessly.  
A diamond in my eye, lucy resting on my lips,  
Now watch this harmonic rip and split—

The fabric bends, space-time rewinds,  
Galactic tracks snap back, elastics unwind.  
Blast it—it’s axiomatic, I’m tactically smashing  
The technocratic advancements with rhythmic enhancements.

Neurochemical nano-technology,  
Symmetry born from chemicals locked inside of me.  
Now my mind floats free, untethered on PCP,  
Breaking down reality geometrically.

A mental recipe: let it be, breathe energy,  
Speak in telepathy, memory of eternity.  
The mystery’s deep, hiding grief beneath,  
Piece apart the darkness, a spark to release.

Bleed and harness what’s haunting the conscious,  
Evil thoughts intrude, but I move undaunted.  
Pharmaceutical illusions fuse with solutions,  
I’m proving truth, hallucinogenic revolutions.

Beyond it—I’m bionic,  
Super-sonic, true harmonic.  
Rock it like a Travis Scott mosh pit—  
Nauseous from the drip hitting my lips.

The back of my throat ignites; now I choke,  
Smoking potent stardust, lungs fill with the hope.  
Every toke cracks the code of the cosmos,  
I’m lost in this phosphorescent hypnosis.

Axiomatic Revelation

Axiomatic excelebration, Excalibur the challenger,  
I'm after mathematics that split tragic from magical—  
Only if you knew what this game could do to you,

---

You’d dive deep, seeking truth where the lies grew.

Shining blinding, my gifts feel undeserved,  
Burning red hot embers, I’m recalling lessons learned.  
The pretender, a piece of the puzzle in disguise,  
I sleep through trouble, my mind stays amplified.

Past lives of robberies and pot flips,  
Bad trips with DMT and cracked whips—  
I never lacked, though hypochondriac,  
Somehow, I act right, knowing that’s a fact.

Behind computer screens, I live unseen,  
A dream architect designing what’s obscene.  
Beneath the trees, frequencies release peace,  
Guarded fiercely, I keep my essence pristine.

Craving Clarity

Craving chips and soda, my energy’s golden,  
Holding the truth bare—no secrecy stolen.  
Frequency encrypted, the leaves share their wisdom,  
Surrounded by love in a broken system.

Maniac falling back, gratitude intact,  
One step at a time—no rewinding the tracks.  
Delusions of society painted with precision,  
But truth isn’t objective—it’s a fractured vision.

Buy low, sell high, whether stocks or supply,  
But this cycle depresses me, so I hide and lie.  
Deep in my zone, the unknown becomes home,  
Private hell echoes as my heartbeat roams.

Chasing Freedom

Hijacked by moments, screaming through prisons,  
I focus on fear, where freedom’s provisioned.  
Hope as a weapon, angel wings clipped—  
Sick coughs echo, lunatics script.

Stocks dipped, sweat wet, Armageddon looms,  
Depression questions while my spirit consumes.  
Ripped into labs, floating with new descriptions,  
Music lifts darkness with sublime prescriptions.

I’m an ego-maniac locked in inferiority’s grip,  
Wrapped in designer truths, skipping counterfeit scripts.  
Between ruthless lunacy and unity’s glow,  
I balance belief, sow seeds, and grow.

Breaking Free

I’m running through tombs into zoom meetings,  
Heart beating, moon chasing sun fleeting.  
Time cracked wide as my flow state arrives,  
Breaking the chains that hypnotized lives.

Truth more than objective, it’s energy shifting,  
Infinite layers align in rhythms uplifting.  
Detached, not disinterested—free of corrupt constructs,  
Spitting ancestral codes while fate conducts.

From Atlantis to the Mariana trench’s abyss,  
My lyrics dive deep, splitting neurons like this.  
Mystic rhythms disrupt hypnotic traditions,  
Manifesting a movement beyond mental prisons.

Resurrection

Resurrected, I’ve befriended past enemies,  
Rebuilt from ruins, aligned with infinity.  
Quantum codes unlocked through ancestral sound,  
I’m an architect of dreams, profound and unbound.

Music flows limitless, breaking untrue designs,  
While every verse redefines this space-time line.  
A young marauder, my pen translates the void,  
Every word a spark, destruction deployed.

Energy rearranged, curses broken by light,  
Patience my weapon, illuminating the night.  
Sonic arrangements crafted in basements,  
Boundaries erased by timeless statements.

Outro

So take this—an artifact of words divine,  
Rhythms that ignite minds and shift time.  
Life’s chaos is perfection, a lesson in disguise,  
Each syllable a bridge to truth realized.

I’m the student and the master, a reflection of the whole,  
Carving paths for others to find their role.  
No gimmicks—just raw, infinite vibration,  
Every verse a blueprint, the key to liberation.

The cultivation of my mental is makin' my music so beautiful,  
Choose to grow, never let it go, the next episode is incredible.  
Unfold the scroll, the story untold, a timeless art,  
I plant these seeds of rhythm, watch the harvest spark.

Highly flammable is the medical bud grown in the unknown,  
Elevated mindstate, golden thoughts carved in stone.  
Shiny, understandable, ethical love shown,  
Pull you back from the void just to guide you home.

Alone in the one zone on my golden throne, I focus,  
Loosen the tightness, unblock the flow—I'm devoted.  
Energetic passages, blockages removed and smoothed,  
Every groove I produce is the proof of inner truths.

Cool and sober at times, though my mind's a wildfire,  
Other days I rhyme slow, nights I burn like a live wire.

---

Fast tracks or slow jams, the beat always lasts,  
Out here with the outlaws, I’m built to surpass.

Yes, I’m that raw, shape-shifting with the facts,  
Malleable wisdom forms the path I craft.  
Proud to show the pathway, leading through the unknown,  
Every bar a step, every verse a milestone.

This ain’t just flow; it’s a road to transcendence,  
Every line I write fights through the tension.  
I mention the lessons, the blessings disguised as scars,  
Every trial and tribulation etched into my bars.

The cultivation of my soul reflects in every note,  
I light the torch of growth, my truth forever spoke.  
From the ether to the speaker, let the echo roam,  
I'm the bridge between the stars and this place I call home.

Puzzle pieces fit in distant arrangements,  
Discover beliefs, let the fragments take new statements.

Trying to find better words, clever ways to profess,  
Through similes, my sentiments carve through this mess.  
Certainly, this excrement is serving me some way unseen,  
It’s too obvious, probably blessed and cursed in-between.

My brain rhymes as I say, “spines shine coldly.”  
I know it doesn't make sense, but flow boldly.  
Keep moving, keep grooving, like you’re floating  
Along a solar ocean, hoping to crack it open.

My mind flies into astral dimensions,  
Letting prevention slip through new intentions.  
Enter the exit, through over-under lessons,  
I meant this, apparent with merit, no questions.

I’ve got a lot of this for myself, none for others,  
So I rediscover mysteries, recovering smothered  
Truths. Apparently, I live miserably when unplugged spiritually,  
Still struggling to get into it—this friction feels eerie to me.

Why is there resistance? Why does it persist?  
I’ve worked through this with a grit that doesn’t fit,  
Non-congruent with the fluid movements of truth.  
It peeks, then retreats, leaving me in the darkness of youth.

Strung out in a cave, unable to behave,  
A devil playing accordion, chaos engraved.  
According to him, I will never win,  
Cursed by the darkest magics that dwell within.

My life’s been tragic, distractions are my hymn,  
Jumping from one to another, but the truth still swims.  
I find this, release this; belief is the trick,  
Uncovered by the discovery of what “me” is.

Aligned with nothing, floating through existence,  
No goals or mission, just a purposeless persistence.  
My heart hurts—it’s truth through the roof,  
I rip off the top and cough up soot.

Vaping on PCP, I epitomize paradoxical victory.  
Losing feels like winning in a twisted symmetry,  
I’m likely nothing but fragments of all of you.  
Falling through rabbit holes, I hold a tragic tune.

I’ve got a grip on the underground’s sound,  
Years of anxiety, my mind’s cleared, now blurred back around.  
The misery creeps again, I feel it crawl,  
Though nothing’s lacking, fear builds walls.

I work through transgressions, integrating shadows,  
For every step forward, two back follows.  
Truth or lie? Subjective lines cross my mind,  
Seeking objective designs, reality defined.

-Verse 2

Shining like a diamond in the rough, kinda messed up,  
Nine designer drugs deep, defiant, stressed but…  
Spending time with defiant thugs on the grind,  
Alkaline resilience, def
